THIRD-PARTY NOTICES for norite-daemon

Norite itself is licensed AGPL-3.0-or-later; see the LICENSE file at the repository root.
This file covers the third-party modules linked into this binary, and nothing else. It is
generated by `just notices` from `go version -m` on the built binary — the linker is the only
authority on what actually ships. Do not edit it by hand; CI regenerates it and fails on a diff.
